OpenAI 模型即将登陆 Google Cloud

OpenAI models are coming to Google Cloud

OpenAI 与 Google Cloud 将把前沿模型和 Codex 工作流引入 Gemini 的 Model Garden，并通过 Google Cloud Marketplace 提供。

英文原文 EN

OpenAI and Google Cloud are bringing frontier models and Codex workflows to Gemini’s Model Garden, available through Google Cloud Marketplace.

洞察这不只是新增销售渠道，而是模型竞争从封闭生态转向跨云分发与工作流占位。Google Cloud 同时承载 Gemini 与 OpenAI，说明企业客户正在要求模型可选、统一治理和可迁移的智能体基础设施；真正的护城河将更多落在开发者体验、评测、治理与企业集成，而非单一模型入口。
招聘行动人才 mapping 应扩大到跨云模型平台、AI Marketplace、企业模型治理与 Codex 类工作流团队，重点识别同时理解云基础设施、模型路由、开发者生态和大型客户交付的人才；招聘画像不应只盯模型训练，也要覆盖能把多模型能力嵌入企业采购与安全体系的平台负责人。

Using thousands of AI agents to uncover new leads in biological data

英文原文 EN

See how OpenAI, PerturbAI, and Scripps Research used distributed AI agents to uncover promising research leads in data from millions of brain cells.

洞察智能体扩展正在从软件任务进入科学发现：规模化的关键不只是增加 agent 数量，而是任务分解、证据追踪、结果聚合和专业验证。生命科学将成为检验智能体系统能否从自动执行迈向可审计知识生产的重要战场，也会推动模型工程与领域科学深度耦合。
招聘行动优先建立计算生物学、单细胞分析、科学智能体编排和科研评测人才池，重点寻找既能设计多智能体系统、又理解实验与数据可信度的人才。mapping 时应关注 OpenAI 科学团队、PerturbAI、Scripps 及相邻的 AI for Science 团队之间的人才流动与联合作者网络。

Mistral 与 NVIDIA 推进工程领域 AI

Mistral Advances AI for Engineering with NVIDIA

Mistral 与 NVIDIA 将 Mistral Large 4、数据生成和物理建模流程，与 CUDA-X、NVIDIA Warp 及 Omniverse 结合，用于加速仿真和闭环工程设计；双方还计划发布物理模型及 CAE、EDA 智能体的公共基准与开放数据集。

洞察通用大模型正在与物理仿真栈合流，工业 AI 的价值链从问答延伸到设计、仿真、验证和迭代闭环。开放基准和数据集若落地，会降低工业智能体的评测门槛，并使 CAE、EDA、数字孪生与 GPU 计算人才成为模型公司争夺的新核心群体。
招聘行动立即扩充 scientific ML、物理建模、CAE、EDA、CUDA、Omniverse 和工业智能体方向的人才地图，优先锁定具备仿真软件与生成模型双重背景的人才。候选人研究应从传统 AI Lab 外扩至 NVIDIA、工业软件厂商、芯片设计工具公司和顶尖工程计算实验室。

Sam Altman 确认 Aleksander Madry 承担超级智能安全工作

I am very grateful Aleksander accepted our request to come take this on; the safety and security challenges in front of us as we confront superintelligence are immense, and he has an excellent set of skills to work on this.

Sam Altman 表示，Aleksander Madry 已接受邀请承担相关工作；面对超级智能，安全与安保挑战十分严峻，而 Madry 具备适合处理这些问题的能力。该帖引用了 Madry 关于回归 OpenAI 的个人更新。

洞察创始人亲自确认关键负责人回归，是组织优先级比一般招聘信息更强的信号。超级智能安全正在从研究议题升级为需要兼顾对抗鲁棒性、系统安全和组织治理的核心岗位，头部实验室会继续围绕少数跨学科领军者重组团队。
招聘行动将 Madry 的学术合作者、前团队成员、学生与产业合作网络纳入重点 mapping，尤其关注对抗鲁棒性、可信机器学习、系统安全和评测交叉背景。后续应持续跟踪其公开研究方向、团队招聘与 OpenAI 内部安全组织边界变化，以识别新的关键岗位和人才迁移。

★ 本周重点Frontier RadarMistral AI产品发布官方频道AI Agent推理产品方向多模态

Voxtral 3：实时语音智能体模型与 SDK

Voxtral 3

Mistral 发布 Voxtral 3 语音模型家族与实时 voice agent SDK，将转录、推理、工具调用和语音合成串联为流式流程。转录覆盖 50 多种语言，实时版本延迟低于 500 毫秒；4B Voxtral Mini Transcribe 3 定价为每分钟 0.0035 美元，并支持欧盟托管 API 或自有基础设施部署。

洞察语音模型的竞争指标已从单点识别准确率转向端到端时延、工具调用、部署主权与单位成本。低于 500 毫秒的交互目标意味着语音智能体正在逼近可替代传统呼叫中心和实时助手栈的体验阈值，欧洲本地化部署也显示数据主权正在成为产品能力的一部分。
招聘行动人才搜寻应覆盖流式 ASR、低延迟推理、语音合成、对话编排和边云协同部署，重点识别做过端到端实时系统而非单一语音模块的人才。mapping 可重点追踪 Mistral、语音基础模型团队、联络中心 AI 公司以及欧洲主权云生态。

发布 Enterprise Frontier Safeguards（EFS）：兼顾隐私与先进安全防护

We’re announcing Enterprise Frontier Safeguards (EFS), a solution that combines the privacy

Anthropic 发布 Enterprise Frontier Safeguards（EFS），将零数据保留的隐私能力与先进的滥用检测防护相结合。EFS 将数据存储在由客户控制的云基础设施中，而非 Anthropic 的环境中。

英文原文 EN

We’re announcing Enterprise Frontier Safeguards (EFS), a solution that combines the privacy of zero data retention with state-of-the-art safeguards for detecting misuse. EFS works by storing data in cloud infrastructure controlled by the customer, not Anthropic.

洞察企业级安全正从模型外围政策转化为可部署的基础设施产品。客户控制数据平面、供应商提供安全能力的模式，会把隐私工程、云安全、检测系统和模型治理合并为新的平台层，也可能成为高监管行业采用前沿模型的前置条件。
招聘行动招聘与 mapping 应把隐私工程、云原生安全、威胁检测、模型滥用防护和合规架构视为一体化人才市场，优先寻找能在客户云中交付安全控制面的人才。可重点跟踪 Anthropic、云安全厂商、数据隔离平台和金融医疗 AI 基础设施团队。

模型的工程语言或可压缩为最小词表

I oddly love the models' engineering vernacular: gate, evidence, culprit, durable, provenance, contract… A lot of these

Vercel CEO Guillermo Rauch 观察到，模型常使用 gate、evidence、culprit、durable、provenance 和 contract 等工程词汇，因为大量工程工作本质上是调试和理解既有系统。他提出，生产力所需的最小英语词表可能远少于简化技术英语标准中的约九百词。

英文原文 EN

I oddly love the models' engineering vernacular: gate, evidence, culprit, durable, provenance, contract… A lot of these stem from most engineering being debugging and understanding existing systems, where you take on the detective / auditor roles. You do forensics. I wonder what the *minimum viable English* in fact is to be productive. ASD-STE100 Simplified Technical English contains ~900 approved words. I suspect we need far fewer.

洞察面向 agent 的技术语言若能标准化和压缩，可能提升提示一致性、跨团队沟通与自动化评测效率。
招聘行动在 agent 工具和开发者体验岗位中，关注能把复杂工程概念抽象为稳定术语、规范和可评测指令的人才。
X / Guillermo Rauch · 2026-10-25 ♥ 219 · ↻ 8 · 💬 91 原文 ↗

Follow Builders社区/其他Builder 推文Builder 声音

网络安全 agent 的 token 用量或增长千倍

We’ll likely have 1,000X more tokens flowing through cyber agents soon than we do today. As the surface area of cyber

Box CEO Aaron Levie 预测，网络安全 agent 很快会消耗比现在多一千倍的 token。agent swarm 可并行调用安全工具、模拟攻击、读取日志并处理企业数据，覆盖身份、网络、代码扫描和数据保护等安全栈。

英文原文 EN

We’ll likely have 1,000X more tokens flowing through cyber agents soon than we do today. As the surface area of cyber risk increases, at some point soon it will be nearly impossible to keep up with the volume of new risks (we’re essentially already there). Large companies barely had the resources to pull this off before, but the vast majority of companies don’t. There’s a never ending set of libraries to keep up with, tools to leverage, and sprawling infrastructure and code to secure. We’ll have agent swarms that can fan out, leverage any security tool, test various perpetuations of attacks, read through logs, process enterprise data for risk level, and more. This capability only emerged in the past few months. Wouldn’t have been possible before long running agents and computer use were good enough, and models hadn’t been trained on cyber deeply. That’s all changing now. This is a huge area of opportunity right now for every layer of the security stack across identity, firewall, network, code scanning, data protection, and more. And it’s at least one reason to be optimistic about the balance of offensive and defensive capability.

洞察长时运行、computer use 与网络安全训练的成熟，使安全从人力受限流程转向可大规模并行的机器工作负载。
招聘行动优先 mapping 自动化红队、SOC agent、安全工具编排、身份与数据保护人才，并关注能控制误报、权限和推理成本的负责人。
X / Aaron Levie · 2026-10-25 ♥ 88 · ↻ 8 · 💬 50 原文 ↗

AI 降低摩擦，也可能带走学习与协作的养分

struggle together i learned programming and design by trying, failing, and trying again. when i hit a wall, i figured

曾任 Cursor、Notion 与 Stripe 设计师的 Ryo Lu 反思，编程和设计能力来自反复失败、求助与共同磨合。agent 能快速给出可用结果，却可能让人跳过理解问题、学习提问和与他人建立信任的过程；他期待工具帮助人进入真实世界，而不是把人困在各自的屏幕里。

英文原文 EN

struggle together i learned programming and design by trying, failing, and trying again. when i hit a wall, i figured out how to climb it. read something i barely understood. took things apart. asked someone for help. started over. when i began my career, i kept doing things i had never done before. trying different ways to make something work. when i stopped learning, i moved on to the next struggle. many things i made weren’t very good. but after enough rounds, something stuck. what once took so much effort became part of how i thought. i could feel something was wrong before i knew how to explain it. it became like water. i think about what went into that. the bug i couldn’t fix. the design that looked right until someone used it. the conversation that made me realize my flaws. now we can ask a machine. i’ve made things with these tools that i might never have made otherwise. i don’t miss every tedious task. but i wonder what happens when we remove the difficulty before we understand what it was teaching us. we ask. something appears. it works well enough. we move on. we learn how to get results without necessarily understanding how it works. and because the result looks complete, it becomes easy to miss the questions we never learned to ask. there is another side to this. working with people is uncomfortable. everyone wants something different. everyone has a history you don’t know. someone cares about a detail you think is unimportant. someone pushes back when you want to move. someone needs time when you feel certain. it takes a while to find each other. but sometimes, after struggling together, you become a band. you know who notices what you miss. who can pull an idea apart without killing it. when to step in, when to slow down, when to let someone else carry the rhythm. you share something unfinished. admit you don’t know. hurt each other, apologize, try again. eventually you make something none of you could have made alone. some of my closest friendships came from working this way. smart, caring humans who saw me confused, wrong, tired, excited. people i could be vulnerable with. people whose ideas changed mine. i worry we are making it easier to avoid that beginning. an agent is always there. patient. ready to keep going. talking to it can feel easier than reaching toward someone who might misunderstand you, disagree, or have somewhere else to be. sometimes that ease helps. but if we keep choosing it, where do the friendships begin? i picture each of us with our own agents. our own explanations. our own little worlds. making more things, with fewer reasons to leave the screen or work through a difference with another person. and these worlds aren’t entirely ours. they run on machines in server farms, owned by companies, shaped by interests we may not see. we bring them our questions, our work, our loneliness. they become part of how we understand what is real. is that freedom? or a comfortable kind of confinement, where everything responds to us but fewer things reach us? i don’t think pain makes something good. there are struggles nobody should have to endure. but i don’t want to lose the difficulty of learning something unfamiliar, or getting to know someone who isn’t like me. maybe we need tools that help us spend more time with the world, instead of giving us fewer reasons to enter it. tools that make it easier to find people, share something unfinished, and work through what we couldn’t figure out alone. sometimes i want to close the laptop. sit with people i love. play something we haven’t figured out yet. miss a note, lose the rhythm, listen until we find each other again. like a jazz band. everyone bringing something different. nobody knowing where we’ll end up. i don’t want a life without struggle. i want people to struggle with. something we can feel, change, and make our own. music we couldn’t have made alone, instead of the silent static of the server farm.

洞察AI 产品若只优化即时完成率，可能损害长期能力积累和团队关系；优秀设计需要同时保留学习反馈与人际协作。
招聘行动招聘 AI 产品与设计人才时，考察其能否识别自动化的隐性代价，并设计需要解释、复盘和多人协同的工作流。

Each agent's state is stored in Postgres, so agents survive restarts and cost nothing while idle. You can message them and stream their events over the API. 英文原文 EN

Cyber is going to be one of the most profound aspects of AI. We now have a tireless workforce that can hunt down, discover, patch, or attack any system and software ever created. We will use agents to identify the long standing risks in existing systems and infrastructure. Attackers will use agents to accelerate the discovery of gaps in environments. We will equally use agents to defend attacks and discover anomalous patterns to better protect our data, and much more. We’re entering a new cyber regime, and we’ll probably use 100X more tokens on this loop than we’re imagining right now, because we can scan every log, look at every line of code, look through traffic, and process data at a scale that wasn’t comprehensible before. More than ever, enterprises are going to care deeply about how they manage their networks, systems, software, data, identity, devices, and more. Agents will largely become the solution to these challenges, but they will keep everyone up in the process.

专用自主系统正在越过 LLM wrapper 阶段

Pretty cool that we clearly have an emerging form factor for what AI might look like for the foreseeable future as auton

Box CEO Aaron Levie 认为，AI 的新形态是面向特定任务或领域的自主系统。更强的工具与 computer use、垂直领域判断，以及 sandbox、文件系统和数据管线等 harness，使 agent 能执行更长、更复杂的企业工作，但仍需人类监督、合适 UX 与变革管理。

英文原文 EN

Pretty cool that we clearly have an emerging form factor for what AI might look like for the foreseeable future as autonomous systems dedicated to specific tasks or domains. This is a huge leap past the LLM wrapper days because the new expectation is that you can finally farm out more sophisticated work to the agent to tackle for you. And you can reliably trust that work can get accomplished properly (still with a human in the loop) because of far better tool and computer use, and the general intelligence of the models improving. 1) The tool and computer use capability increases in the models mean that you can provide the agent with far more granular access to external data systems and other software to operate. This means, in effect, the agent begins to execute workflows similar to how a person would have in that same function. And where there isn’t existing software, the agent just builds it itself. This is a huge unlock. 2) Next, the actual capability of the models are improving in just about every vertical domain. The models can make far better judgment calls in legal, do deeper bio research, scan for security issues in code, understand supply chain processes, and more. Obviously in many of these spaces then models still have worse intuition than people, but that will only get better. 3) Finally, the harnesses and surrounding infra — sandboxes, file systems, data pipelines, etc. — allows for longer running agents, agents to be able to work on arbitrarily complex or long tasks, process data at enormous scale, and more. Few of these components worked well for AI even a year ago, but we now have many of the core primitives necessary for more autonomous systems. When combined with the right UX, implementation, and change management for the human oversight and coordination, it’s clear that these autonomous systems will become quite powerful in a wide variety of enterprise workflows. We’re starting to see them in IT automation, cyber operations, drug discovery, manufacturing, AI research, and more spaces. You can almost imagine one or more of these systems for every role in the economy. Quite a bit of opportunity ahead.

招聘行动按网络安全、生命科学、制造和 IT 自动化等领域 mapping agent 工程与领域专家，优先寻找做过生产级变革落地的人。

通过热力学奇异性检测扩散模型中的记忆现象

Detection of Memorization in Diffusion Models Through Thermodynamic Singularity

论文摘要（作者自述，未经独立复现）：在扩散模型中，正确的分布学习需要逆转基于底层密度函数的扩散过程，而一种关键失效是记忆现象：逆向过程转而学习由单个训练样本出发的扩散。我们对这类发生记忆的逆向过程给出解析刻画，并提出检测相关热力学奇异性的判据。在一系列旨在促进或抑制记忆的神经网络模型中，该判据能可靠区分正确学习的网络与发生记忆的网络。

英文原文 EN

In diffusion models, proper distributional learning entails reversing a diffusion process over underlying density functions, whereas a key failure is memorization, in which the reverse process instead learns diffusion from individual training samples. We provide an analytical characterization of such memorized reverse processes, along with a criterion that detects the associated thermodynamic singularity. The proposed criterion reliably distinguishes between properly learned and memorizing networks across a range of neural network models designed to promote or mitigate memorization.

Wrote an OC-plugin for this "smart goal" feature.

Follow Builders社区/其他Builder 推文Builder 声音

模型越强，垂直 AI 系统的差异化反而越大

The big bitter lesson question in the applied AI layer is that as models get better and better, what is the best way to

Box CEO Aaron Levie 认为，早期模型能力有限，应用只能做 prompt 与 RAG wrapper；如今 agent 能长时运行、调用工具、协调 swarm 并接入领域数据，安全、生命科学、医疗、金融和制造等垂直系统反而拥有更大专业化空间。

英文原文 EN

The big bitter lesson question in the applied AI layer is that as models get better and better, what is the best way to diffuse that intelligence into an enterprise. Ironically, I’d argue that as models are getting more capable, and can handle longer running tasks and better tool use, the ability to differentiate on a domain by domain basis goes up instead of down. When models were quite limited in what they could do, ironically all you could be literally was a “wrapper” because you were effectively delivering tokens back to the user with a custom prompt or some limited RAG. But in a world of agents using tools, tasks that run on for hours or days, workflows that kick off agents, agent swarms that need to be coordinated against a goal, data sources that are domain specific, model routing that requires domain-specific routing, implementation that requires real change management of the workflow instead of “just type into this chat box”, it’s very very possible that specialization of these systems goes up more. Obviously this doesn’t apply to everything - the closer you are to the labs core use-cases the tougher this play gets, but I’d argue we’re going to see autonomous systems for a wide range of job functions that weren’t practical to build out before. We’ll see this in security, life sciences, healthcare, finance, manufacturing, and many other fields. Either way going to be a fun and wild couple of years.

洞察前沿模型能力商品化不会自动消灭应用护城河，领域工作流、数据、路由、实施和变革管理会变得更重要。
招聘行动按垂直领域建立 AI 人才地图，优先寻找兼具领域知识、agent 架构和企业实施经验的复合型负责人。

Wabi 2.0 用编排器与临时主题对话重构个人 agent

A few weeks ago, I was ranting about multiplayer AI experiences and that's when @ekuyda shared an early preview of Wabi

Nikunj Kothari 认为，Wabi 2.0 把个人 agent 的形态做对了：主 agent 负责编排，系统主动生成单一目的的主题对话，可与他人共享，并在旅行或报税等任务结束后归档。他的主要批评是产品过于主动，应用体验仍需改进。

英文原文 EN

A few weeks ago, I was ranting about multiplayer AI experiences and that's when @ekuyda shared an early preview of Wabi 2.0.. Truth be told, I was one of the early users of Wabi 1.0 and while the design was gorgeous, it frankly never "stuck" with me. But, in moments of downloading this edition, I was immediately hooked. It just gets the "paradigm" right imo. There are two types of personal agents today: 1) Singleplayer iMessage or Muse types - while useful it's hard to compartmentalize them when many things are going on 2) Build your own multiple chat Hermes agents - while that's useful and can share context, they live forever Wabi has this great paradigm of orchestrator (which is the main agent) and topic specific episodic chats that are for a single purpose. You can share these individual chats with folks (for example the school one with my wife). And these are generated proactively AND don't need to stay forever (which is important). For example, for my recent NY trip, it was generated based on a flight it saw, provided help where needed, and then after the trip was done, it was archived. Same with my 2025 taxes. My only critique so far it's TOO proactive, the apps can be better and they need to dial both of those in, but this is a wonderful product that everyone should try! (I know it's shocking to see this in today's day and age, but this is neither paid, nor an investor, just a fan)

洞察临时、可共享、可归档的任务空间比永久聊天线程更适合个人 agent，也更容易控制上下文和隐私。
招聘行动关注 agent orchestration、episodic memory、多人共享与 proactive UX 人才，并重点评估主动程度的可配置性。
X / Nikunj Kothari · 2026-10-19 ♥ 46 · ↻ 6 · 💬 7 原文 ↗

agent swarm 把研究与测试转化为算力约束问题

Here’s why agent swarms are going to be a very big deal. We can finally make compute a critical variable in a large numb

Box CEO Aaron Levie 认为，许多研究、数据处理、发现和测试任务长期受人类规划与协调能力限制。能调用工具、写代码、处理数据并彼此协作的 agent swarm，可把网络安全、生命科学、材料研究、交易和 QA 等任务大规模并行化。

英文原文 EN

Here’s why agent swarms are going to be a very big deal. We can finally make compute a critical variable in a large number of important tasks in the economy which wasn’t possible before. There’s a large amount of work in the world that is essentially only constrained by the amount of time that we put into research, data processing, discovery work, running tests, and more. All of this work is bottlenecked by our ability to plan, execute, review, and coordinate everything happening in these workflows. Which means we basically do very little of them today relative to their economic value. Agent swarms are the first real ability to parallelize this work in a way where - in theory - the only parameter that matters is how much compute you throw at the problem. Cyber security, life sciences or material science research, trading, QA testing, data processing, and many other fields are essentially compute constrained problems in the limit once models are good enough at these tasks. The breakthrough is that in a world where agents can use tools, write code, process data, have strong domain depth, and can coordinate with each other, you can begin to solve arbitrarily complex problems by setting the right goals and applying enough tokens to the problem. This changes a lot of fields as well as even the competitive dynamics in these fields.

洞察当认知劳动可并行扩展后，竞争优势会转向目标设定、编排、验证和可投入算力，而非单纯增加人手。
招聘行动重点 mapping swarm orchestration、并行评测、科学计算和成本调度人才，并设计能验证协作效率的复杂任务。
X / Aaron Levie · 2026-10-19 ♥ 121 · ↻ 13 · 💬 44 原文 ↗

Jev 把 AI 重点从生成文本转向做决策

Jev: The AI That Makes Decisions, Not Text - and Why It Changes Everything (Diogo Almeida)

核心观点：Typesafe CEO Diogo Almeida 认为，自动化不能只依赖大多数时候聪明、偶尔失误却不自知的模型，关键是让系统知道何时不可靠。Jev 强调 intelligence per dollar，而不只是 intelligence per second；团队以低调研发和用户实测形成口碑，Almeida 直言 AI 既令人惊叹，也令人失望，并用「我们在打造欺诈，不是在造神」提醒外界关注可用性而非神话。

洞察从生成模型走向决策模型后，置信度、成本、校准和失败暴露会成为企业采用的核心指标，单纯追求更大模型不足以支撑自动化。
招聘行动重点 mapping 决策模型、模型校准、低成本推理和风险控制人才，并在面试中要求候选人说明系统如何识别并升级不确定决策。

Leash Bio 访谈：用真实实验闭环识别模型捷径

Lab-in-the-Loop at Compute Speed — Ian Quigley, Leash Bio

Ian Quigley 的约110分钟访谈介绍 DNA 编码化合物库、未见化合物与蛋白突变测试，以及将实验测量转为科学强化学习环境。与 Orchestra Bio 的研发运营方向互补，重点 mapping 实验数据、模型偏差诊断和化学智能体交叉人才；摘要依据官方节目笔记，非逐字引语。

英文原文 EN

这期约110分钟的访谈围绕 Leash Bio 的 DNA 编码化合物库实验与 AI 建模展开。官方节目笔记介绍，团队为同一化合物配置多个 DNA 标识，以区分真实分子结合与蛋白对 DNA 标签的非特异性吸附；通过未见化合物库和蛋白突变测试，排查模型利用化学家身份、蛋白标识或已有结构数据等捷径。笔记还介绍 Hermes 使用 Leash 自产实验数据训练，以及 Minos 将实验测量转为可重复运行的科学强化学习环境，让智能体在有限实验预算下探索结构与活性关系。采集依据为官方节目笔记及音频元数据，不将笔记表述视为逐字引语。

Reflection AI 用 Beam 押注美国开放权重前沿模型

Beam: The Great American Open Model with ReflectionAI Co-Founder and CEO Misha Laskin

核心观点：Reflection AI 联合创始人兼 CEO Misha Laskin 认为，开放模型不仅关乎可访问性，也能让更多研究者发现安全问题，他引用 Linus 定律强调「足够多的眼睛会让大多数安全漏洞变浅」。团队约一年内从三十人扩展到约三百人，搭建预训练、中期训练、强化学习、数据与算力基础设施，并发布首个开放模型 Beam；他强调成功不是某个秘诀，而是人才、文化、数据、算力和基础设施等三十件事都要做对。

洞察从零建设前沿模型实验室需要完整组织能力，开放权重竞争会同时拉动 RL、训练系统、数据和安全研究人才需求。
招聘行动更新 Reflection AI 与 Beam 核心团队 mapping，重点覆盖前 DeepMind、Gemini、AlphaGo、预训练、RL 与大规模基础设施人才。
